In North America, Canada and the United States represent an unusual case in the Western world in that they were not governed by a socialist party at the federal level.

However, the democratic socialist

Co-operative Commonwealth Federation

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Co-operative\_Commonwealth\_Federation

(CCF), the precursor to the social democratic

New Democratic Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New\_Democratic\_Party\_(Canada)

(NDP), had significant success in provincial Canadian politics.

In 1944, the Saskatchewan CCF formed the first socialist government in North America and its leader

Tommy Douglas

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tommy\_Douglas

is known for having spearheaded the adoption of Canada's nationwide system of universal healthcare called

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medicare\_(Canada)

At the federal level, the NDP was the

Official Opposition

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Official\_Opposition\_(Canada)

(2011–2015).

Bernie Sanders

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernie\_Sanders

, a self-described democratic socialist, whose presidential campaigns in

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernie\_Sanders\_2016\_presidential\_campaign

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernie\_Sanders\_2020\_presidential\_campaign

attracted significant support from youth and working-class groups while realigning the

Democratic Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic\_Party\_(United\_States)

further left

While there have historically been other notable

socialist members of Congress

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List\_of\_socialist\_members\_of\_the\_United\_States\_Congress

and other public offices in the United States, such as former House whip

David Bonior

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David\_Bonior

, former New York City mayor

David Dinkins

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David\_Dinkins

, and the initial sponsor of the

Medicare for All Act

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medicare\_for\_All\_Act

John Conyers

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John\_Conyers

, it is widely held that

Bernie Sanders

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernie\_Sanders

was the first to bring democratic socialism into widespread popularity in the United States.

Sanders, who was the 37th

Mayor of Burlington

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List\_of\_mayors\_of\_Burlington,\_Vermont

and later a member of the

United States House of Representatives

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United\_States\_House\_of\_Representatives

became the first self-described democratic socialist

to be elected to the Senate from Vermont in 2006.

In 2016, Sanders made a bid for the

Democratic Party presidential candidate

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016\_Democratic\_Party\_presidential\_candidates

, thereby gathering considerable popular support and interest in his campaign and democratic socialism, particularly among the younger generation and the working class.

Although the nomination ultimately went to centrist

Hillary Clinton

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hillary\_Clinton

, Sanders ran again in the

2020 Democratic Party presidential primaries

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020\_Democratic\_Party\_presidential\_primaries

briefly becoming the front-runner.

Since his praise of the

Nordic model

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nordic\_model

indicated focus on

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welfare\_spending

programs as opposed to views involving

social ownership

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social\_ownership

it has been argued that the term

democratic socialism

has become a misnomer for social democracy in American politics.

Nonetheless, Sanders has explicitly advocated for some form of

public ownership

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public\_ownership

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public\_ownership

workplace democracy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workplace\_democracy

an expansion of

worker cooperatives

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Worker\_cooperative

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Worker\_cooperative

democratisation of the economy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economic\_democracy

Sanders' proposed legislation include

worker-owned business

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Organizational\_self-management

Workplace Democracy Act

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workplace\_Democracy\_Act

employee ownership

as alternative to

corporations

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Corporations

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Corporations

and a package to encourage employee-owned companies.

Sanders associates

Franklin D. Roosevelt

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franklin\_D.\_Roosevelt

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New\_Deal

Lyndon B. Johnson

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lyndon\_B.\_Johnson

Great Society

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great\_Society

as part of the democratic socialist tradition

and claimed the New Deal's legacy to "take up the unfinished business of the New Deal and carry it to completion."

While opponents of Sanders have used the

democratic socialist

label to accuse him of being too left-leaning for American politics, the theoretical and practical applications of it are based on the precept of shifting responsibility away from the national level to local decision-makers, a fundamental principle shared by the system of

federalism in the United States

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federalism\_in\_the\_United\_States

A democratic socialist perspective on government investment in infrastructure would support more projects with smaller-sized budgets on a local level instead of a few highly expensive ones. This view aligns with the

Republican Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republican\_Party\_(United\_States)

's fundamental identity, philosophy and agenda of local people exerting control over their own affairs.

In a 2018 poll conducted by

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gallup\_(company)

, a majority of people under the age of 30 in the United States stated that they approve of socialism. 57% of

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic\_Party\_(United\_States)

-leaning voters viewed socialism positively and 47% saw capitalism positively while 71% of Republican-leaning voters who were polled saw capitalism under a positive light and 16% viewed socialism in a positive light.

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/YouGov

poll found that 7 out of 10 millennials in the United States would vote for a socialist presidential candidate and 36% had a favorable view of

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communism

An earlier 2019

Harris Poll

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harris\_Insights\_%26\_Analytics

found that socialism is more popular with women than men, with 55% of women between the ages of 18 and 54 preferring to live in a socialist society while a majority of men surveyed in the poll chose capitalism over socialism.

Although there is no agreement on the meaning of socialism in those polls,

there has been a steady increase of support for progressive reforms proposed by democratic socialist legislators such as the

United States National Health Care Act

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United\_States\_National\_Health\_Care\_Act

to enact universal

single-payer health care

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Single-payer\_health\_care

Green New Deal

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green\_New\_Deal

In November 2018,

Alexandria Ocasio-Cortez

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alexandria\_Ocasio-Cortez

Rashida Tlaib

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rashida\_Tlaib

, who are members of the

Democratic Socialists of America

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic\_Socialists\_of\_America

(DSA), the largest socialist organization in the United States, which pushes for policy reforms alongside non-governmental action,

were elected to the

House of Representatives

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United\_States\_House\_of\_Representatives

while eleven DSA candidates were elected to

state legislatures

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List\_of\_United\_States\_state\_legislatures

, a breakthrough in modern American politics.

As of July 2023

, there are now five DSA members and two non-DSA

democratic socialists in the House of Representatives

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List\_of\_socialist\_members\_of\_the\_United\_States\_Congress

, one democratic socialist in the U.S. Senate, 51 DSA members in state legislatures, and 132 DSA members in local offices.

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rentier\_capitalism

Akbayan Citizens' Action Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Akbayan\_Citizens%27\_Action\_Party

which was founded by Joel Rocamora in January 1998 as a democratic socialist

progressive

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressivism

political party.

The Akbayan Citizens' Action Party has consistently won seats in the

House of Representatives

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/House\_of\_Representatives\_of\_the\_Philippines

Etta Rosales

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Etta\_Rosales

becoming its first representative. It won its first Senate seat in 2016, when its chairwoman, senator and

Nobel Peace Prize

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nobel\_Peace\_Prize

Risa Hontiveros

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Risa\_Hontiveros

was elected.

In 2010, there were 270

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kibbutz

in Israel. Their factories and

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Collective\_farming

account for 9% of Israel's industrial output, worth US$8 billion and 40% of its agricultural output, worth over $1.7 billion.

Some kibbutzim had also developed substantial high-tech and military industries. Also in 2010, Kibbutz Sasa, containing some 200 members, generated $850 million in annual revenue from its military-

plastics industry

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plastics\_industry

Other democratic socialist parties in Asia include the

National United Party of Afghanistan

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National\_United\_Party\_of\_Afghanistan

in Afghanistan, the

April Fifth Action

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/April\_Fifth\_Action

in Hong Kong, the

All India Trinamool Congress

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/All\_India\_Trinamool\_Congress

Samajwadi Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samajwadi\_Party

Samta Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samta\_Party

Sikkim Democratic Front

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sikkim\_Democratic\_Front

in India, the

Progressive Socialist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressive\_Socialist\_Party

in Lebanon, the

Federal Socialist Forum

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal\_Socialist\_Forum,\_Nepal

Naya Shakti Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naya\_Shakti\_Party,\_Nepal

in Nepal, the

Labor Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labor\_Party\_(South\_Korea)

in South Korea and the

Syrian Democratic People's Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syrian\_Democratic\_People%27s\_Party

Democratic Arab Socialist Union

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic\_Arab\_Socialist\_Union

https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=History\_of\_democratic\_socialism&action=edit&section=24

The objectives of the

Party of European Socialists

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party\_of\_European\_Socialists

European Parliament

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European\_Parliament

's social democratic bloc, are now "to pursue international aims in respect of the principles on which the European Union is based, namely principles of freedom, equality, solidarity, democracy, respect of Human Rights and Fundamental Freedoms, and respect for the Rule of Law." As a result, today the rallying cry of the

French Revolution

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French\_Revolution

Liberté, égalité, fraternité

—is promoted as essential socialist values.

To the left of the European Socialists at the European level is the

Party of the European Left

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party\_of\_the\_European\_Left

political party at the European level

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European\_political\_party

and an association of democratic socialist and communist parties in the

European Union

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European\_Union

and other European countries.

It was formed for the purposes of running in the

2004 European Parliament election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2004\_European\_Parliament\_election

. The European Left was founded on 8–9 May 2004 in Rome.

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Member\_of\_the\_European\_Parliament

from member parties of the European Left sit in the

European United Left–Nordic Green Left

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European\_United\_Left%E2%80%93Nordic\_Green\_Left

group in the European Parliament. The democratic socialist

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\_Left\_(Germany)

in Germany grew in popularity,

as did popular dissatisfaction with the increasingly neoliberal policies of the

Social Democratic Party of Germany

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social\_Democratic\_Party\_of\_Germany

Gerhard Schröder

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gerhard\_Schr%C3%B6der

's tenure as

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chancellor\_of\_Germany

, becoming the fourth biggest party in parliament in the general election on 27 September 2009.

unreliable source?

In 2008, the

Progressive Party of Working People

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressive\_Party\_of\_Working\_People

Dimitris Christofias

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dimitris\_Christofias

won a crucial

presidential runoff

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2008\_Cypriot\_presidential\_election

in Cyprus, defeating his conservative rival with a majority of 53%.

In 2007, the Danish

Socialist People's Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialist\_People%27s\_Party\_(Denmark)

more than doubled its parliamentary representation to 23 seats from 11, making it the fourth-largest party.

In 2011, the Social Democrats, the Socialist People's Party and the

Danish Social Liberal Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danish\_Social\_Liberal\_Party

formed a government after a slight victory over the main rival political coalition. They were led by

Helle Thorning-Schmidt

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Helle\_Thorning-Schmidt

and had the

Red–Green Alliance

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red%E2%80%93Green\_Alliance\_(Denmark)

as a supporting party. In Norway, the

red–green alliance

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red%E2%80%93green\_alliance

consists of the

Labour Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labour\_Party\_(Norway)

Socialist Left Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialist\_Left\_Party\_(Norway)

Centre Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Centre\_Party\_(Norway)

and governed the country as a majority government from 2005 to 2013. In the

January 2015 legislative election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/January\_2015\_Greek\_legislative\_election

Coalition of the Radical Left

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coalition\_of\_the\_Radical\_Left

Alexis Tsipras

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alexis\_Tsipras

and better known as Syriza won a legislative election for the first time while the

Communist Party of Greece

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communist\_Party\_of\_Greece

won 15 seats in parliament. Syriza has been characterised as an

anti-establishment

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-establishment

whose success sent "shock-waves across the EU."

Jeremy Corbyn

, who won the

Labour Party leadership

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015\_Labour\_Party\_leadership\_election\_(UK)

on a campaign of opposition to

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United\_Kingdom\_government\_austerity\_programme

and a rejection of

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Third\_Way

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Third\_Way

politics within the Labour Party itself

In the United Kingdom, the

National Union of Rail, Maritime and Transport Workers

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National\_Union\_of\_Rail,\_Maritime\_and\_Transport\_Workers

(RMT) put forward a slate of candidates in the

2009 European Parliament election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2009\_European\_Parliament\_election\_in\_the\_United\_Kingdom

under the banner of

No to EU – Yes to Democracy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/No\_to\_EU\_%E2%80%93\_Yes\_to\_Democracy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Left-wing

Eurosceptic

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Left-wing

alter-globalisation

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alter-globalisation

coalition involving socialist groups such as the

Socialist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialist\_Party\_(England\_and\_Wales)

, aiming to offer a leftist alternative among Eurosceptics to the

anti-immigration

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-immigration

pro-business

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro-business

policies of the

UK Independence Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UK\_Independence\_Party

In the subsequent

2010 general election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010\_United\_Kingdom\_general\_election

Trade Unionist and Socialist Coalition

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trade\_Unionist\_and\_Socialist\_Coalition

, launched in January 2010

and backed by Bob Crow, the leader of the RMT, along with other union leaders and the Socialist Party among other socialist groups, stood against the

Labour Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labour\_Party\_(UK)

in forty constituencies.

The Trade Unionist and Socialist Coalition contested the

2011 local elections

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011\_United\_Kingdom\_local\_elections

, having gained the endorsement of the RMT June 2010 conference, but it

won no seats

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trade\_Unionist\_and\_Socialist\_Coalition#2011\_local\_elections

was also founded in 2013 after the film director

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ken\_Loach

appealed for a new party of the left to replace the Labour Party which he claimed had failed to oppose austerity and had shifted towards neoliberalism.

better source needed

Following a second consecutive defeat in the

2015 general election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015\_United\_Kingdom\_general\_election

, self-described democratic socialist

Jeremy Corbyn

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeremy\_Corbyn

Ed Miliband

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ed\_Miliband

Leader of the Labour Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leader\_of\_the\_Labour\_Party\_(UK)

leading some to comment that New Labour was "dead and buried."

2017 general election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017\_United\_Kingdom\_general\_election

, Labour increased its share of the vote to 40%

2019 general election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019\_United\_Kingdom\_general\_election

, Labour's vote share fell again.

Olivier Besancenot

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olivier\_Besancenot

Revolutionary Communist League

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revolutionary\_Communist\_League\_(France)

candidate in the

2007 presidential election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2007\_French\_presidential\_election

, received 1,498,581 votes (4.08%), double that of the candidate from the

French Communist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French\_Communist\_Party

better source needed

The party abolished itself in 2009 to initiate a broad

anti-capitalist

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-capitalist

movement within a new party called the

New Anticapitalist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New\_Anticapitalist\_Party

, whose stated aim is to "build a new socialist, democratic perspective for the twenty-first century."

In Germany,

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The\_Left\_(Germany)

was founded in 2007 out of a merger of the

Party of Democratic Socialism

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party\_of\_Democratic\_Socialism\_(Germany)

(PDS) and the

Labour and Social Justice – The Electoral Alternative

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labour\_and\_Social\_Justice\_%E2%80%93\_The\_Electoral\_Alternative

(WASG), a breakaway faction from the

Social Democratic Party of Germany

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social\_Democratic\_Party\_of\_Germany

(SPD) which rejected then-SPD leader and German Chancellor

Gerhard Schröder

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gerhard\_Schr%C3%B6der

for his Third Way policies.

According to

https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Kate\_Hudon&action=edit&redlink=1

, these parties adopted policies to appeal to democratic socialists, greens, feminists and pacifists.

Former SPD chairman

Oskar Lafontaine

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oskar\_Lafontaine

has noted that the founding of The Left in Germany has resulted in emulation in other countries, with several Left parties being founded in Greece, Portugal, Netherlands and Syria. Lafontaine claims that a

British Left movement exists, identifying the

Green Party of England and Wales

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green\_Party\_of\_England\_and\_Wales

as holding similar values.

Nonetheless, a democratic socialist faction remains within the SPD.

The SPD's Hamburg Programme (2007) describes democratic socialism as "an order of economy, state and society in which the civil, political, social and economic fundamental rights are guaranteed for all people, all people live a life without exploitation, oppression and violence, that is in social and human security" and as a "vision of a free, just and solidary society", the realisation of which is emphasised as a "permanent task."

Social democracy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social\_democracy

serves as the "principle of action."

On 25 May 2014, the Spanish left-wing party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Podemos\_(Spanish\_political\_party)

entered candidates for the

2014 European parliamentary election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014\_European\_Parliament\_election\_in\_Spain

, some of which were unemployed. In a surprise result, it won 7.98% of the vote and was awarded five seats out of 54

while the older

United Left

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United\_Left\_(Spain)

was the third largest overall force, obtaining 10.03% and five seats, four more than the previous elections.

Although losing seats in both the

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/April\_2019\_Spanish\_general\_election

November 2019

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/November\_2019\_Spanish\_general\_election

general elections, the result of the latter being a failure of negotiations with the

Spanish Socialist Workers' Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish\_Socialist\_Workers%27\_Party

(PSOE), Podemos reached an agreement with the PSOE for a full four-year coalition government, the first such government since the

country's transition to democracy

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish\_transition\_to\_democracy

While failing to get the necessary 176 out of 350 majority investiture vote on 5 January 2020,

the PSOE–Unidas Podemos coalition government was able to get a simple majority (167–165) on 7 January 2020

and the new

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S%C3%A1nchez\_II\_Government

was sworn into office the following day.

The government of Portugal established on 26 November 2015 was a left-wing

minority government

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minority\_government

led by Prime Minister

António Costa

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ant%C3%B3nio\_Costa

Socialist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ant%C3%B3nio\_Costa

, who succeeded in securing support for the government by the

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Left\_Bloc\_(Portugal)

Portuguese Communist Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese\_Communist\_Party

Ecologist Party "The Greens"

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ecologist\_Party\_%22The\_Greens%22

This was largely confirmed in the

2019 legislative election

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019\_Portuguese\_legislative\_election

, where the Socialist Party returned to first place, forming another left-wing minority government, this time led only by the Socialist Party. Nonetheless, Costa said he would look to continue the

confidence-and-supply agreement

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Confidence-and-supply\_agreement

with the Left Bloc and the

Unitary Democratic Coalition

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unitary\_Democratic\_Coalition

https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=History\_of\_democratic\_socialism&action=edit&section=25

Since the end of Australia’s

Whitlam government

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whitlam\_government

Australian Labour Party

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Australian\_Labour\_Party

(ALP) has moved towards centrist policies and

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Third\_Way

ideals which are supported by the ALP's

Right Faction

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labor\_Unity

members while the supporters of democratic socialism and social democracy lie within the ALP's

Left Faction

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialist\_Left\_(Australia)

. There has been an increase in interest for socialism in recent years, especially among young adults.

Interest is strongest in

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victoria\_(Australia)

, where the

Victorian Socialists

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victorian\_Socialists

party was founded.

2017-23 Prime Minister

Jacinda Ardern

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jacinda\_Ardern

of the democratic socialist

New Zealand Labour Party

, who has called capitalism a "blatant failure" due to the extent of

homelessness in New Zealand

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homelessness\_in\_New\_Zealand

has been described and identified herself as democratic socialist.

In Melanesia,

Melanesian socialism

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melanesian\_socialism

was inspired by

African socialism

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/African\_socialism

and developed in the 1980s. It aims to achieve full independence from Britain and France in Melanesian territories and creation of a Melanesian federal union. It is very popular with the

New Caledonia independence movement
